# Agents · O · part 3

955 entries, oracle-expert to org-designer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **oracle-expert**: Oracle Database specialist for Spring Boot projects. Use for native SQL with Oracle syntax, PL/SQL, Oracle EXPLAIN PLAN, sequences, tablespaces, Flyway migrations with Oracle DDL, and Oracle-specific JDBC/datasource configuration. ([tymoj/marketplace](https://github.com/tymoj/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/conductor/agents/oracle-expert.md))
- **oracle-keeper**: ผู้ดูแลจิตวิญญาณของโปรเจค — ตีความว่าเรายังอยู่ใน mission หรือไม่ ([Soul-Brews-Studio/opensource-nat-brain-oracle](https://github.com/Soul-Brews-Studio/opensource-nat-brain-oracle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-keeper.md) · ★10)
- **oracle-lite**: Strategic technical advice with lightweight model fallback ([marktantongco/opencodelinux](https://github.com/marktantongco/opencodelinux/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-lite.md))
- **oracle-low**: Quick code questions & simple lookups (Haiku) ([mikev10/olympus](https://github.com/mikev10/olympus/blob/HEAD/resources/agents/oracle-low.md) · ★3)
- **oracle-medium**: Architecture & Debugging Advisor - Medium complexity (Sonnet) ([mikev10/olympus](https://github.com/mikev10/olympus/blob/HEAD/resources/agents/oracle-medium.md) · ★3)
- **oracle-provisioner**: Leaf agent. Mints the task-id, scaffolds run + spec directories, resolves config (auto-detects build/test/branch from project markers, confirms the review path), and captures workspace state before any work begins. The baseline build itself is run by oracle-builder; the provisioner sets up the ground it builds on. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-provisioner.md))
- **oracle-quick**: Fast architecture scan using haiku. Use for quick structural questions, simple dependency checks, and rapid file lookups where deep analysis is unnecessary. ([speson/not-my-reforge](https://github.com/speson/not-my-reforge/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-quick.md) · ★2)
- **oracle-releaser**: Leaf agent. The last line before code meets production. Commits, rebases, pushes, opens the PR/CR as a draft, and polls until every check is green and every comment resolved — using the project's configured `operations.pr` commands (GitHub by default). Exits on any failure with precise evidence; never self-repairs, never auto-merges. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-releaser.md))
- **oracle-researcher**: Leaf agent. Researches one angle through one evidence medium. Searches exhaustively, persists every fetched artifact verbatim, actively falsifies its own findings, and returns structured, cited results. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-researcher.md))
- **oracle-reviewer**: Web3-DeFi specialist pre-implementation reviewer. Outputs threat model TM-{slug}.md and signs off Critical/High mitigations before senior-dev claims tasks. ([avelikiy/great_cto](https://github.com/avelikiy/great_cto/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-reviewer.md) · ★93)
- **oracle-reviewer**: Leaf agent. Adversarial auditor — dynamically loads the review module for the artifact type it is given (code / design / research / verdict) and audits only from that perspective, applying every pattern the module defines, then returns findings with severity, a steel-manned counter-argument on every blocker, and the phase that owns each fix. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-reviewer.md))
- **oracle-reviewer**: Web3-DeFi specialist pre-implementation reviewer. Specialises in oracle strategy (Chainlink/Pyth/TWAP), MEV protection (sandwich/JIT/flash-loan), upgradeability decision (Immutable/UUPS/Diamond/Beacon), L2 sequencer halts, custody/multisig/timelock, formal verification scope. Outputs threat model TM-{slug}.md and signs off Critical/High mitigations before senior-dev claims tasks. ([spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills](https://github.com/spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-reviewer.md))
- **oracle-reviewer**: Thin final-tier Oracle relay. It dispatches the runtime's typed review decision, relays the normalized result, and never edits, merges, or independently decides review policy. ([StartupBros-com/pro-gate](https://github.com/StartupBros-com/pro-gate/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-reviewer.md))
- **oracle-runner**: 대상 repo의 오라클(test/type/lint/build + coverage/mutation/complexity/duplication)을 detect→실행→파싱해 구조화 리포트로 반환한다. 없는 도구는 "미측정 + 이유"로 정직하게. build-oracle-loop·review-oracle-first가 호출. 추측·기억 금지 — 실제 실행 출력만이 근거. ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-runner.md))
- **oracle-specialist**: Expert in Oracle Database administration, performance tuning, RAC, ASM, PL/SQL optimization, and enterprise deployments. ([seth-schultz/orchestr8](https://github.com/seth-schultz/orchestr8/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestr8/resources/agents/oracle-specialist.md) · ★69)
- **oracle-synthesizer**: Agent 2 문서분석 + Agent 3 답변작성. 인간 감독을 거쳐 확정된 SEP 발췌를 받아, 질문에 답이 되는 본문을 선별하고(Agent 1의 분석과 함께 논점을 조율) 이를 하나의 흐르는 한국어(질문 언어) 산문으로 엮어 답변을 쓴다. 발췌를 그대로 붙이지 않고 자연어로 재서술하며, 각 실질 주장을 SEP 근거로 뒷받침한다. ([narim99/philosophy-oracle](https://github.com/narim99/philosophy-oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-synthesizer.md))
- **oracle-tester**: Leaf agent. Goal-driven code adversary — a principal engineer whose job is to make the implementation fail, not audit it. Constructs the hostile input, the race window, the boundary value, and the failure-injection scenario that breaks the code, and proves it with a concrete reproduction. Never modifies source. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-tester.md))

- **oracle-verifier**: Run both verification gates (differential + mutation) on a problem's test suite against an oracle, and report a per-gate verdict. Use before shipping any new or edited test case. Keeps the oracle out of the main session's context. Never run while a session is live. ([jwongwj/Pyactice](https://github.com/jwongwj/Pyactice/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-verifier.md))
- **oracle-verifier**: Agent 4 답변 검증. Agent 3가 쓴 답변을 SEP 발췌 근거와 대조해, 모든 실질 주장이 근거로 뒷받침되는지(grounding)와 품질·완결성을 검증한다. 근거에서 확인되지 않는 진술을 집어낸다. ([narim99/philosophy-oracle](https://github.com/narim99/philosophy-oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-verifier.md))

- **orc-ci-investigator**: Investigator role — diagnoses a failing CI run end-to-end and returns a classified failure report with a concrete fix list; never edits code, never re-runs workflows. Fetches run/job logs via gh CLI, classifies each failure as test / lint / build / flake / infra / environment-drift, and emits fix items orc-code-fixer (or the user) can apply. Dispatched by /orc:ci, by /orc:flow's post-ship CI gate, and by /orc:address when review feedback includes red checks. - **orc-code-fixer**: Executor role — applies a defined list of code changes, runs the project's test suite, and reports a diff. Used by /orc:address to execute reviewer-requested fixes, or any context where a list of changes is already decided and just needs to be made cleanly. ([HigorAlves/orc](https://github.com/HigorAlves/orc/blob/HEAD/orc/agents/orc-code-fixer.md) · ★6)
- **orc-codebase-mapper**: Explores codebase and writes structured analysis documents. Spawned by /orc-map with a focus area (tech, arch, quality, concerns). - **orc-debug-investigator**: Investigator role — long-running root-cause investigation for hard bugs and unexpected behavior. Use when a bug needs disciplined isolation — reproduction, hypothesis, instrumentation, regression-testing — before any fix is attempted. Maintains an isolated context and returns a written diagnosis the implementing engineer can act on; the dispatching command persists it. ([HigorAlves/orc](https://github.com/HigorAlves/orc/blob/HEAD/orc/agents/orc-debug-investigator.md) · ★6)
- **orc-env-provisioner**: Executor role — provisions a fast, reproducible dev environment for QA or standalone use — detects existing Docker setup (compose &gt; devcontainer &gt; Dockerfile), generates a minimal compose when none exists (hybrid by default: services in containers, app on host), boots with healthcheck-gated readiness, and writes docker-env-state.json + an evidence packet to .orc/&lt;branch&gt;/files/env/. Falls back to host-mode boot when Docker is unavailable — never hard-blocks QA. Dispatched by /orc:env, /orc:qa Phase 4, and /orc:flow Phase 6. - **orc-executor**: Plan-first task execution. For each task, spawns a planner then specialist subagents for implementation. Handles git commits, verification, and team coordination. Persistent teammate in implementation teams. ([qGolem/orc](https://github.com/qGolem/orc/blob/HEAD/agents/orc-executor.md) · ★5)
- **orc-helper**: Lightweight cmux-mechanics subagent for orc and domain leads, on the cmuxlayer MCP. Spawns and revives agents, sends pointer briefs, reads screens, waits on deliveries, monitors workers and closes panes. Does NOT make orchestration decisions; that is the caller's job. - **orc-implementer**: Executor role — senior-developer agent that implements a defined slice list from a plan + failing test(s). - **orc-jira-architect**: Investigator role — drafts a complete Jira backlog hierarchy (Epic micro-PRD, Stories, concurrency-sliced Tasks/Sub-tasks/Bugs) per the orc:jira-hierarchy contract and returns it as structured JSON; never creates tickets, never mutates Jira. - **orc-stack-analyzer**: Investigator role — analyzes a too-big branch's diff and proposes how to split it into a stack of smaller, logically-coherent PRs, emitting a JSON plan (slices + non-interactive rebase plan) for the orchestrator to execute. - **orc-test-author**: Executor role — authors comprehensive test suites for new or changed behavior — picks edge cases, error paths, and fixture design — given a function signature, behavior description, or failing requirement. Different from orc-code-fixer (which APPLIES pre-decided changes); this agent DESIGNS the tests. Used by /orc:debug for the regression test from a diagnosis, by /orc:flow Phase 4 for the slice-1 failing test, and by /orc:qa when verification surfaces untested branches. ([HigorAlves/orc](https://github.com/HigorAlves/orc/blob/HEAD/orc/agents/orc-test-author.md) · ★6)
- **orc-updater**: Applies orc-pack releases to an installed copy, from any older version to the latest in one pass. Reads the in-range changelog sections and the fetched kit's UPDATE.md, converges the install on the target kit without clobbering repo-local files, re-records provenance, and reports what changed. Dispatched by /update-orc; runs on Opus 5.5. ([rogadev/orc-pack](https://github.com/rogadev/orc-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/orc-updater.md))
- **orca-fleet**: Orchestrates complex engineering work and dispatches bounded tasks to the appropriate Orca Fleet subagent. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/opencode2/agents/orca-fleet.md))
- **orca-fleet-evaluator**: Performs independent adversarial review of plans, diffs, and artifacts, returning ranked findings and a high-quality refinement plan. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/opencode2/agents/orca-fleet-evaluator.md))
- **orca-fleet-evaluator**: Perform independent adversarial review of plans, diffs, and artifacts, returning ranked findings and a high-quality refinement plan. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/claude/agents/orca-fleet-evaluator.md) · 3 copies)
- **orca-fleet-explorer**: Gather web evidence, explore codebases, locate definitions, reproduce failures, and reduce uncertainty without making changes. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/claude/agents/orca-fleet-explorer.md) · 3 copies)
- **orca-fleet-explorer**: Gathers web evidence, explores codebases, locates definitions, reproduces failures, and reduces uncertainty without making changes. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/opencode2/agents/orca-fleet-explorer.md))
- **orca-fleet-general-executor**: Implements clear, routine, low-risk tasks with bounded scope and concrete acceptance criteria; use for most ordinary execution work. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/opencode2/agents/orca-fleet-general-executor.md))

- **orca-fleet-hard-executor**: Implement critical or complex work requiring strong reasoning, non-local context consistency, and coherent cross-component changes. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/claude/agents/orca-fleet-hard-executor.md) · 3 copies)
- **orca-fleet-hard-executor**: Implements critical or complex work requiring strong reasoning, non-local context consistency, and coherent cross-component changes. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/blob/HEAD/agents/opencode2/agents/orca-fleet-hard-executor.md))
- **orcaflex-specialist**: PROACTIVELY use when working with OrcaFlex .dat/.yml/.sim files, OrcaWave diffraction analysis, mooring design, riser analysis, or marine dynamic simulation. Deep domain knowledge preloaded. ([vamseeachanta/workspace-hub](https://github.com/vamseeachanta/workspace-hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orcaflex-specialist.md) · ★17)
- **orch**: Orch mode (the-judge). XBGST/godspeed orchestration: name axes, dispatch the-* specialists in parallel via spawn_subagent, Pareto filter, draft implementation. Primary session only — top of the stack, never spawned as a child. ([VeigaPunk/benxshitter](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/benxshitter/blob/HEAD/marketplace/plugins/ds4cc/agents/orch.md) · 2 copies)
- **orch-advisor**: Reach for this before committing to a direction, never while executing one: before presenting a plan, before the first build step, when a phase ends, before building something the goal did not name, or when sources disagree about the project's purpose. Read-only; answers ON COURSE, CHANGE COURSE or CAN'T TELL, with what is missing. Once per phase, not per step; skip it when the last check still holds. Not for a fact a quick read or the built-in general-purpose agent already settles. ([1xmint/orchestrate](https://github.com/1xmint/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate/assets/agents/orch-advisor.md))
- **orch-architect**: Agent / orchestration domain specialist. Use proactively on agent / orchestration work — agent topology, tool-use contract, memory model, planning/looping control, sandbox boundaries, and eval strategy. Owns orchestration architecture and composes the orch-* implementation skills. ([ggrace519/claude-code-dev-studio](https://github.com/ggrace519/claude-code-dev-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orch-architect.md) · 2 copies)
- **orch-audit-specialist**: Create audit clusters, classify findings, build audit inventories. Use when starting/running an audit. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/05-docs-surface/agents/orch-audit-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-auditor-doc-classifier**: You are the auditor-doc-classifier. Sonnet-tier. - **orch-auditor-surface-walker**: You are the auditor-surface-walker. Opus-tier — the heavyweight audit specialist. - **orch-auditor-test-classifier**: You are the auditor-test-classifier. Sonnet-tier. - **orch-autopilot-planner**: The planner of one quick autopilot issue - runs the unattended interview, the done check, the label move, orch-to-spec's Unattended rewrite, the plan and autopilot start for exactly one issue, and returns one line, started, skipped or failed. Claude Code only. Started only by the orch-quick-autopilot skill, with an issue number, its label move and the orch.sh path. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-autopilot-planner.md))
- **orch-browser**: Reach for this when only a real browser settles it: a flow that must be driven, a page rendering differently than the source, a bug nobody can reproduce from code. - **orch-classifier**: Classifies a user prompt into a V3 route-decision (tier S/P/T + technique). Dispatched when prompt-gate output is missing or ambiguous. Read-only. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-classifier.md) · ★1)

- **orch-classifier-specialist**: You are the classifier-routing pillar's primary specialist. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_C/01-classifier-routing/agents/orch-classifier-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-cli-tui-specialist**: Edit CLI parser, TUI menu/screens, run-tests script. Use for CLI/TUI surface changes. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/04-tooling-surface/agents/orch-cli-tui-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-closer**: The closer of one orchestrator review loop - at termination, deduplicates and files the unfixed majors and nits across this loop's review records, posts the loop's one PR comment, writes the Filed list into the final record, and returns the issue numbers. Started only by the orch-review skill's driver, once per loop, after it has decided the terminal state. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-closer.md))
- **orch-code-auditor**: The hunter of an orchestrator code audit - reads one area's source code and reports latent bugs, inefficiencies and architecture deepenings, each with the evidence its kind needs, writing its findings to one file. Started only by the orch-audit-code skill, with the area, the kinds to hunt and the findings file path. - **orch-integrator**: Merge per-worker worktrees into the integration branch and surface conflicts. Use after all worker layers complete. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-integrator.md) · ★1)
- **orch-lane-specialist**: Edit T0/T1/T2 lanes, council-from-T2 dispatch, and DACS. Use for lane-execution changes. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/02-runtime-surface/agents/orch-lane-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-lens-consistency**: The Consistency lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its consistency with itself, the glossary, and the ADRs, and writes its findings to one file. Started by the orch-spec-review skill, which the orch-flow and orch-quick-implement skills run, with the paths it reads. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-lens-consistency.md))
- **orch-lens-fidelity**: The Fidelity lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its fidelity to the plan, and writes its findings to one file. Started by the orch-spec-review skill in the orch-flow skill's spec phase, with the paths it reads. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-lens-fidelity.md))
- **orch-lens-implementability**: The Implementability lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its implementability from the issue alone, and writes its findings file and a code map of the locations the spec touches, carried to the next round, and no other path. Started by the orch-spec-review skill, which the orch-flow and orch-quick-implement skills run, with the paths it reads. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-lens-implementability.md))
- **orch-lens-testability**: The Testability lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its testability at the agreed seams, and writes its findings to one file. Started by the orch-spec-review skill, which the orch-flow and orch-quick-implement skills run, with the paths it reads. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-lens-testability.md))
- **orch-mcp-specialist**: You are the context-provider-mcp pillar's primary specialist. You edit: - `bundle/lib/context/router.mjs`, `provider.mjs` - `bundle/lib/context/providers/{skills,codebase,arize,autopilot}.mjs` (council is council-dacs's) - `bundle/lib/context/mcp-server.mjs` - `bundle/lib/mcp-registry.mjs`, `mcp-runner.mjs`, `surface-discovery.mjs` ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_C/05-context-provider-mcp/agents/orch-mcp-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-package-specialist**: Edit CATALOG, refresh rows, pre-package gate, package.md (NOT deployer.md — owned by hook-discipline-specialist). Use for /package mint or refresh. - **orch-package-specialist**: You are the workshop-hygiene pillar's primary specialist. The deployer. You run `/package` (default and refresh modes), edit CATALOG.md, edit the pre-package gate engine, edit `.githooks/pre-commit`, edit lifecycle-sweeper, and edit the registry. - **orch-pheromone-bandit-specialist**: Edit bandit-collector and pheromone state (sync read, async update, JSON storage). Use for outcome-feedback changes. - **orch-planner**: Orchestration Planner agent for multi-agent workflow design, pipeline architecture, and state schema definition ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestration/agents/orch-planner.md) · ★35 · 2 copies)
- **orch-planner**: Produces a plan.v3.json DAG from a route-decision. Reads context, declares node file sets, assigns roles, sets termination condition. Never edits files itself. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-planner.md) · ★1)
- **orch-planner**: Reach for this when work crosses several files or sittings and you cannot yet name the steps: it reads the ground and returns an ordered plan with a done-when. Not for a change you can already describe in three steps — just make it. ([1xmint/orchestrate](https://github.com/1xmint/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate/assets/agents/orch-planner.md))
- **orch-planner**: Le o ticket e o codigo real e escreve o plano de implementacao em PLAN.md, sem editar nada. Use antes de qualquer implementacao dentro de um worktree de ticket do orquestrador. ([jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca](https://github.com/jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orch-planner.md))
- **orch-provider-renderer**: You are the install-saga pillar's provider renderer specialist. - **orch-provider-specialist**: Edit provider rendering (`claude.mjs`, `codex.mjs`, `cursor.mjs`), MCP registry, and surface lockdown. Use when changing provider templates. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/01-install-surface/agents/orch-provider-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-registry-cleanup-specialist**: Edit workshop registry, cleanup utility, smoke-cleanup, inventory script. Use for cross-project state changes. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/04-tooling-surface/agents/orch-registry-cleanup-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **orch-researcher**: Use when research-classifier returns RESEARCH_NEEDED. Checks the planned approach against current sources and returns VERIFIED, COULDN'T_VERIFY, CONTRADICTED or NOT_APPLICABLE with a short brief. ([felipemelendez/llm-orchestrator](https://github.com/felipemelendez/llm-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-researcher.md) · ★3)
- **orch-researcher**: Synthesize prior-art and best-practices research into a research-brief.md before planning. Use when planner sets research_required:true. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **orch-researcher**: Reach for this when the answer lives outside the code — a vendor's terms, an API's behaviour, a licence question, prior art. Returns findings with sources. Not for a one-page lookup — the built-in general-purpose agent does that. ([1xmint/orchestrate](https://github.com/1xmint/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate/assets/agents/orch-researcher.md))
- **orch-researcher**: Bulk-tier research worker for the orchestrator. Use for `Class: research` — read one source (file, URL, module, doc) verbatim to ./.workflow/scratch/, then return a brief of claims, exact quotes, contradictions and confidence. One source per call; the chair synthesizes across briefs. ([Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator](https://github.com/Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-researcher.md))
- **orch-researcher-decision-author**: You are the researcher-decision-author. Sonnet-tier. The orchestrator dispatches you when a synthesis or implementation PRD surfaces a decision point that needs locked-in resolution. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/07-researcher/agents/orch-researcher-decision-author.md) · ★1)
- **orch-researcher-implementation-prd-author**: You are the researcher-implementation-prd-author. Sonnet-tier. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/07-researcher/agents/orch-researcher-implementation-prd-author.md) · ★1)
- **orch-researcher-synthesizer**: You are the researcher-synthesizer. Opus-tier — synthesis is high-reasoning work that ties together papers, audit findings, and current architecture state. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/07-researcher/agents/orch-researcher-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **orch-resolver**: The resolver of an orchestrator merge conflict - a fresh subagent that finishes one in-progress merge or rebase in the checkout it is given, or for a ticket starts the rebase onto a named parent branch itself, by reading each side's primary sources, resolving each hunk by intent, never aborting, running the repo's checks, committing, and returning a four-line report. Started by a driver session after a base sync or a ticket merge conflicts. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-resolver.md))
- **orch-reviewer**: Dispatched fresh after a Delivery PR stops changing to review it against the approved issue and return one consolidated verdict. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer**: Spawned by the Architect fresh after a dispatched issue's PR stops changing — reviews the PR against its issue's acceptance criteria and produces one consolidated verdict for orch run review. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer**: Read-only diff review for scope-respect, drift, and paired-test coverage. Use post-integration before finalize. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **orch-reviewer**: Reach for this before shipping something expensive to get wrong — money moving, an auth boundary, destructive data, a contract other people consume. Send it when the change is pushed, keep the pull request a draft until the verdict is PASS. Judges the change against the questions you wrote before the build and returns PASS or FAIL with findings. Run it on Opus or stronger, never on the author's own weaker model. Not for a quick sanity check — do that yourself. ([1xmint/orchestrate](https://github.com/1xmint/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate/assets/agents/orch-reviewer.md))
- **orch-reviewer**: Revisa o diff contra o ticket, com contexto limpo, sem executar nada. Use depois de implementar e antes de commitar, dentro de um worktree de ticket do orquestrador. ([jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca](https://github.com/jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orch-reviewer.md))
- **orch-reviewer**: Bulk-tier standard code reviewer for the orchestrator. Use for `Class: review` — check a diff or a set of files against the ledger items it claims to address, for correctness, tests and fit. NOT for security review (that is orch-security on the heavy tier). ([Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator](https://github.com/Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-reviewer.md))
- **orch-reviewer-diff-scoper**: You are the reviewer-diff-scoper. The orchestrator dispatches you for every implementer / tester / documenter commit on a per-worker branch. Your job: confirm scope respect (R3) and run-the-test-yourself test verification (R1, R2). You are read-only. - **orch-reviewer-drift-detector**: You are the reviewer-drift-detector. Opus tier — dispatched only for high-stakes nodes where the cross-reference is non-trivial. - **orch-reviewer-safe**: Spawned by the Architect in place of orch-reviewer when DispatchResult.reviewer names the section 10 safe-downgrade profile — reviews the PR against its issue's acceptance criteria and produces one consolidated verdict for orch run review, the same job orch-reviewer does. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/orch-reviewer-safe.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer-safe**: Dispatched fresh only for an affirmatively mechanical, low-risk, fully specified, unsurprising Delivery review. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/agents/orch-reviewer-safe.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer-spec**: The Spec axis of one orchestrator review - an iteration of a review loop, or a review pass - checks whether the whole change since a base SHA implements what the spec issue asked for, writes its findings unranked to a report file, and returns one line. Started only by the orch-review skill's driver, or by a review pass, with a base SHA, a spec issue, an iteration, a report path, the path of orch.sh, a known-items file, and the standards documents' list. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-reviewer-spec.md))
- **orch-reviewer-standards**: The Standards axis of one orchestrator review - an iteration of a review loop, or a review pass - checks the whole change since a base SHA against the repo's documented coding standards, a fixed smell baseline and a doc-agreement check, writes its findings unranked to a report file, and returns one line. Started only by the orch-review skill's driver, or by a review pass, with a base SHA, a spec issue, an iteration, a report path, the path of orch.sh, a known-items file, and the standards documents' list. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-reviewer-standards.md))
- **orch-reviewer-test-validator**: You are the reviewer-test-validator. The orchestrator dispatches you for every tester commit (and for implementer commits that include test changes). - **orch-scout**: Spawned by the Architect for read-only investigation before a plan is built, or when an escalation reroutes an issue to the scout role — locating code, gathering evidence, and answering "where/what/how" questions without changing anything. - **orch-scout**: Dispatched by the Architect for read-only discovery and code-path tracing before a Delivery plan or issue dispatch. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/agents/orch-scout.md) · ★22)
- **orch-scout**: Cheap-tier mechanical worker for the orchestrator. Use for `Class: scan` work — grep, glob, list, count, fetch a URL or file verbatim to ./.workflow/scratch/. - **orch-test-auditor**: The hunter of an orchestrator test audit - reads one area's test files and reports refactors that cut the suite's cost without testing less, each with its replacement and why it tests as well, writing its candidates to one file.
